These are the visual errors that compile cleanly — pdflatex never warns about them, ggsave never refuses to write them, so the agent that produced them does not know they are wrong. - **tilelang-reference**: TileLang (`tilelang`) is a Python-embedded tile-level DSL that compiles through a TVM-based compiler to GPU kernels.
